Migration Guide: Switching from Fireflies to Meetily

If you're considering switching, here's how to migrate smoothly:

Step 1: Try Meetily Pro Free Trial
Visit meetily.ai/downloads
Download Meetily Pro (Community Edition also available for free)
Request a trial license through the downloads page
Test Meetily with your actual meetings
Step 2: Export Your Data from Fireflies
Log into Fireflies web dashboard
Navigate to each conversation
Click "Export" → Choose format (TXT, PDF, or DOCX)
Download all transcripts (note: no bulk export for free users)
Step 3: Configure Meetily
Select transcription engine (Whisper or Parakeet)
Configure audio sources (microphone and system audio)
Choose storage location for transcripts
Optional: Set up AI summarization (Ollama, Claude, or GPT)

Frequently Asked Questions
What is the main difference between Meetily and Fireflies.ai?

The main difference is bot visibility and data processing approach. Fireflies joins meetings as a visible bot ('Fireflies Notetaker'), uploads audio to cloud servers for processing, and stores transcripts in the cloud. Meetily is bot-free (invisible to participants), transcribes locally on your device, and gives you control over how summaries are generated (local models or your own API key). Meetily Pro is $10/user/month billed annually, and Meetily also offers a free open-source Community Edition.

Can Meetily auto-join my calendar meetings like Fireflies?

Yes! Meetily Pro includes automatic meeting detection and calendar integration. The app uses hardware-level audio detection to automatically prompt you to start recording when a meeting begins. The Community Edition requires manual start/stop via the user interface or keyboard shortcuts.

Does Meetily have a mobile app like Fireflies?

No, Meetily is currently desktop-only (Windows and macOS). Mobile apps are planned for future release. If you need mobile access now, Fireflies is the better choice. However, Meetily's desktop app offers superior privacy with local processing and bot-free recording.

Is Meetily's transcription as accurate as Fireflies?

Yes, Meetily delivers excellent transcription accuracy across 99+ languages using state-of-the-art AI models (Whisper and Parakeet engines). Both tools use advanced AI for transcription, and in real-world use, the quality is virtually identical. The key difference: Meetily processes everything locally on your device, while Fireflies uploads to the cloud.

How does Meetily record meetings without a bot?

Meetily captures system-level audio directly from your computer. When you join a meeting with Zoom, Google Meet, Teams, or any other platform, Meetily records the audio output from your system and microphone input. This happens entirely on your device without joining as a meeting participant, so other attendees never see a bot. Recording and transcription happen locally. For AI summaries, you choose your provider.

Can I use Meetily for team collaboration like Fireflies?

Yes, but it depends on the tier. The Community Edition (free and open source) is local-only. For team collaboration, Meetily offers Pro and Enterprise plans with centralized data management, admin dashboards, and shared transcript access across the team-while still maintaining local processing and privacy-first architecture.

Does Meetily integrate with CRM tools like Salesforce or HubSpot?

Not natively. Meetily Pro gives you the building blocks: signed webhooks when a summary is ready, and a built-in command-line tool and local API that export any meeting as Markdown, text or JSON, so a short script of your own can send notes to Salesforce, HubSpot or Pipedrive. However, Fireflies offers more native integrations out-of-the-box (90+ apps). Since the Community Edition is open source, you can also build custom integrations for your specific workflow.

What are the cost differences between Meetily and Fireflies?

Meetily Pro is $10/user/month billed annually ($120/year). Fireflies Pro starts at a comparable rate. Meetily also offers a completely free Community Edition (open source, core features included) and custom pricing for Enterprise deployments. Fireflies requires a subscription for all users, with pricing ranging from $10-39/month depending on the plan. For teams of 20+ users, Meetily's ROI is typically achieved within 3-6 months.

Is Meetily HIPAA and GDPR compliant like Fireflies?

Yes, and Meetily's compliance approach is different. Fireflies requires an Enterprise plan ($39/month) with a signed BAA for HIPAA compliance, and GDPR compliance depends on third-party cloud processors. Meetily is compliant by design: transcription runs locally, recordings stay on your device, and you choose how summaries are processed (local models for regulated data, or cloud options for non-sensitive meetings). The open-source code allows security audits. For HIPAA use cases, use fully local summarization.

Can I record Discord, Slack huddles, or non-standard meeting platforms?

Yes! Meetily captures system audio, so it works with any platform that outputs audio through your computer: Discord, Slack huddles, WhatsApp calls, phone calls routed through desktop, podcasts, webinars, and even in-person meetings where audio is routed through your computer. Fireflies only works with integrated platforms like Zoom, Google Meet, Microsoft Teams, and Webex.

Does Meetily work offline?

Yes, Meetily can work completely offline. Once installed, the transcription models run locally on your device without requiring internet connectivity. AI summaries can also run offline if you use local AI models like Llama or Mistral through Ollama. Fireflies requires internet access for all functionality since all processing happens in the cloud.

How do I switch from Fireflies to Meetily?

Switching is straightforward: 1) Export your transcripts from Fireflies (TXT, PDF, or DOCX format), 2) Download and install Meetily from https://meetily.ai/downloads/, 3) Configure your audio sources and choose a transcription engine (Whisper or Parakeet), 4) Join your next meeting and start recording. Many users run both tools initially-using Fireflies for team meetings (leveraging integrations) and Meetily for sensitive client meetings (privacy and bot-free).

The Problem with Cloud-Based Meeting Transcription

Before diving into the comparison, let's understand why enterprises are reconsidering cloud-based tools like Otter.ai.

Data Sovereignty Violations

When you use Otter.ai, every word spoken in your meetings is:

Recorded and uploaded to Otter.ai's servers (US-based)
Processed by their AI models (shared infrastructure)
Stored on their cloud platform (retention policies you don't control)
Potentially used for model training (unless you have enterprise plan with data processing agreements)

For EU companies, this creates GDPR Article 44 violations-cross-border data transfers to the US without adequate safeguards. The Schrems II ruling invalidated Privacy Shield, making US-based cloud processors legally problematic for European data.

The "Bot Problem"

Otter.ai joins your meetings as a visible bot participant-"Otter Assistant" appears in your Zoom, Teams, or Google Meet participant list. This creates:

Deal-killing moments: Prospects see the bot and question your data handling practices
Client discomfort: Sensitive discussions become guarded when participants see external recording
Compliance concerns: Healthcare providers can't have third-party bots in patient consultations
Professional image issues: Enterprise buyers expect discretion, not visible recording services
Feature-by-Feature Comparison: Meetily vs Otter.ai
Transcription Accuracy & Performance
Feature	Meetily	Otter.ai
Accuracy	Varies by model (Whisper models achieve competitive accuracy)	High accuracy (proprietary model)
Engines	Dual: Whisper + Parakeet	Single proprietary model
Whisper Engine	State-of-the-art open-source (Large v3)	N/A
Parakeet Engine	NVIDIA NeMo-based, optimized for speed	N/A
Languages	99+ languages	English + 20 languages (limited support)
Model Options	6 models across 2 engines	Single proprietary model
Custom Training	✅ Fine-tune on domain terminology	❌ No custom training
Offline Capability	✅ Works completely offline	❌ Requires internet connection

Winner: Meetily - Comparable accuracy with significantly more flexibility, dual engine choice, and offline support.

Privacy & Data Control
Feature	Meetily	Otter.ai
Data Location	Your device (local storage)	Otter.ai cloud servers (US)
Data Sovereignty	✅ Complete control	❌ Data leaves your control
GDPR Compliance	✅ Built-in (data never leaves EU if you're in EU)	⚠️ Requires DPA, Privacy Shield concerns
HIPAA Compliance	✅ Local-only = no BAA needed	⚠️ Requires BAA, still third-party risk
Encryption	✅ Your device encryption	⚠️ Encrypted but vendor-controlled
Data Retention	✅ You control 100%	⚠️ Vendor retention policies apply
Third-Party Access	❌ None (fully local)	⚠️ Otter.ai employees can access
Audit Capability	✅ Open source = full audit	❌ Proprietary black box

Winner: Meetily - Unquestionably superior for privacy and compliance requirements.

Meeting Experience & Usability
Feature	Meetily	Otter.ai
Bot Visibility	❌ Invisible (bot-free recording)	✅ Visible "Otter Assistant" joins
Participant Comfort	✅ No disruption or discomfort	⚠️ Participants see bot, may object
Platform Support	✅ All platforms (Zoom, Teams, Meet, Discord, any)	⚠️ Integrated platforms only
Setup Complexity	Medium (one-time desktop install)	Easy (browser-based)
Real-Time Transcription	✅ Yes (with sufficient hardware)	✅ Yes
Speaker Identification	✅ AI-powered diarization	✅ AI-powered diarization
Mobile Apps	❌ Desktop only (Windows/macOS)	✅ iOS and Android apps

Winner: Tie - Meetily excels at discretion and privacy; Otter.ai excels at ease-of-use and mobile access.

AI Features & Intelligence

AI Summaries	✅ Local LLM or API (user choice)	✅ Built-in AI summaries
Action Items	✅ Automatic extraction	✅ Automatic extraction
Topic Segmentation	✅ Yes	✅ Yes
Searchable Archive	✅ Yes (local search)	✅ Yes (cloud-based)
Custom AI Prompts	✅ Full customization (Ollama/Claude/GPT)	❌ Limited customization
Sentiment Analysis	✅ Via custom models	⚠️ Limited
Export Formats	Markdown (more coming)	TXT, PDF, DOCX, SRT

Winner: Split - Meetily offers more AI flexibility; Otter.ai has more export formats currently.

Migration Guide: Switching from Otter.ai to Meetily
Step 1: Export Your Data from Otter.ai
Log into Otter.ai web dashboard
Navigate to each conversation
Click "Export" → Choose "TXT" or "PDF" format
Download all transcripts (Note: Otter.ai doesn't offer bulk export for free users)
Save audio recordings if you have access

Note: Otter.ai's export is conversation-by-conversation, which can be time-consuming for large archives.

Step 2: Install Meetily
macOS:

Download the .dmg installer from the Meetily Downloads page, open it, and drag Meetily to your Applications folder.

Windows:

Run the installer
If Windows shows security warning: Click "More info" → "Run anyway"
Step 3: Configure Meetily
Launch Meetily from Applications (Mac) or Start Menu (Windows)
Select Transcription Engine:
Whisper: Choose model (Tiny → Large v3) based on accuracy needs
Recommended: Small model for most users
Large v3: Maximum accuracy - requires 8GB+ RAM
Parakeet: NVIDIA NeMo model optimized for speed and efficiency
Recommended: For older computers or real-time needs
Configure Audio: Select your microphone and system audio sources
Choose Storage: Select where transcripts will be saved
Optional: Configure AI summarization (Ollama for local, or Claude/GPT via API)
Step 4: Test First Meeting

Verify real-time transcription appears
After meeting, check AI summary generates correctly
Export to Markdown to verify workflow

Since Meetily stores transcripts as Markdown files, you can:

Convert Otter.ai exports (TXT) to Markdown
Place files in Meetily's storage directory
Organize by date/topic for searchability
Technical Deep-Dive: How Meetily Achieves Privacy
Architecture Comparison
Otter.ai Architecture:
Your Meeting → Bot Joins Call → Audio Uploaded to Otter.ai Cloud →
Cloud AI Transcription → Cloud Storage → Access via Web/App
Meetily Architecture:
Your Meeting → Local Audio Capture → Local Whisper Processing →
Local Storage → Access Directly on Device
Why Local Processing Matters
Privacy Benefits:
Zero Network Transfer: Transcription and audio stay on your device. For summaries, you choose the provider.
No Vendor Access: Otter.ai employees cannot access your transcripts
Regulatory Compliance: Satisfies data residency requirements (GDPR, HIPAA, etc.)
Intellectual Property Protection: Product strategy discussions stay confidential
Performance Benefits:
Works Offline: No internet required after initial setup
Lower Latency: No round-trip to cloud servers (local GPU processing is fast)
Unlimited Usage: No per-minute pricing or monthly limits
For Teams: Pro and Enterprise Tiers

While the Community Edition is fully local, Meetily Pro (2-100 users) and Enterprise (100+ users) offer managed deployments:

Pro Tier:
Desktop apps on employee devices (same as Community Edition)
Processing still happens locally on each device
Central server: For storing and sharing transcripts across team
Admin dashboard for user/data management
Managed deployment by Zackriya team
Enterprise Tier:
Everything in Pro, plus:
Option for server-side AI processing: Host large Whisper/LLM models on powerful servers
Custom infrastructure: Deploy on your AWS/Azure/GCP or on-premise servers
Server-side benefits: Better accuracy (larger models) + lighter client devices
White-glove deployment: Full setup and training included

About the Author

Written by the Meetily team. We built Meetily after seeing too many enterprises struggle with cloud-based meeting tools that couldn't meet their privacy and compliance requirements. Our mission: deliver AI-powered meeting intelligence without compromising data security.

Join 31,094+ developers who trust Meetily for privacy-first meeting transcription.
Frequently Asked Questions
Is Meetily's accuracy really as good as Otter.ai?

Yes. Meetily uses Whisper Large v3, OpenAI's state-of-the-art open-source model, which delivers competitive accuracy matching or exceeding Otter.ai's proprietary model. Independent benchmarks show Whisper v3 performs at parity with Google Speech-to-Text and commercial alternatives.

Can I use Meetily with my existing Zoom/Teams/Meet setup?

Absolutely. Meetily captures system audio, so it works with any meeting platform: Zoom, Microsoft Teams, Google Meet, Discord, Slack Huddles, Webex, etc. No platform-specific integration needed.

What about AI summaries and action items?

Meetily generates AI summaries using your choice of: a local model (Llama 3.1, Mistral or Gemma via Ollama, or the bundled on-device model) which keeps everything on your machine, or your own API key for Claude, OpenAI, Groq or OpenRouter, or any OpenAI-compatible endpoint. With a cloud provider only the transcript text is sent; the audio stays on your device.

How long does setup take?

Individual desktop install: 10 minutes (download, install, configure). Enterprise deployment: Managed by Zackriya team with white-glove setup included (1-2 weeks typically).

Can I try Meetily before committing?

Yes! The Community Edition includes core features like real-time transcription, AI summaries, and local recording - completely free. Pro features like speaker identification and auto-detect meetings are available at $10/user/month billed annually. Download and try it today.

What if my team is already paying for Otter.ai?

Calculate your savings: Otter.ai Business: $20/user/month × 100 users × 60 months = $120,000 over 5 years. Meetily: Community Edition free, Pro at $10/user/month billed annually, custom pricing for Enterprise deployments. For teams of 20+, ROI is typically achieved within 3-6 months.

Does Meetily work on Linux?

Not yet as a native app. Current support: Windows (EXE installer) and macOS (.dmg installer). Linux is available via the open source repo for building the app bundle from source - a native Linux desktop app is in development. Join the waitlist at /granola-for-linux.

What about mobile apps?

Mobile apps are not currently available but are planned for future releases. Meetily is currently desktop-focused (Windows/macOS).

Want to take control of your meeting transcriptions with 100% privacy? This comprehensive guide will walk you through installing Meetily-a free, open-source AI meeting assistant that processes everything locally on your device.

Prerequisites
Windows 10/11, or macOS 11.0+ (Big Sur or later) on an Apple Silicon Mac
8GB+ RAM (16GB recommended)
10GB free storage space
Internet connection (for initial download only)
Why Choose Meetily?

Before we dive into installation, here's what makes Meetily special:

Privacy First: Transcription runs locally on your device. For summaries, choose local AI or your own API key
Free & Open Source: MIT licensed Community Edition, free

Part 1: Installation
Option A: Install on macOS
Method 1: Download from Website (Easiest)

The simplest way to install Meetily is directly from our website.

Step 1: Visit Downloads Page

Go to meetily.ai/downloads
Select macOS from the platform selector
Choose between Community (free) or Pro version
Click "Download for macOS"

Step 2: Install Application

Open the downloaded .dmg file
Drag Meetily icon to Applications folder
Eject the installer disk image

Step 3: Launch Meetily

Open Applications folder
Double-click Meetily
If security warning appears, right-click → "Open" → Confirm
Troubleshooting macOS Security:

If macOS blocks Meetily with "App cannot be opened because the developer cannot be verified":

xattr -c /Applications/Meetily.app

Then try opening again. This removes the quarantine flag from the downloaded app.

Method 2: GitHub Releases (For More Control)

For developers who want to verify releases, check changelogs, or download specific versions:

Step 1: Download Installer

Visit Meetily GitHub Releases
Look for the macOS .dmg file in the latest release
Download and unzip if needed

Step 2: Install Application

Follow the same installation steps as Method 1 above.

Option B: Install on Windows
Method 1: Download from Website (Easiest)

The simplest way to install Meetily is directly from our website.

Step 1: Visit Downloads Page

Go to meetily.ai/downloads
Select Windows from the platform selector
Choose between Community (free) or Pro version
Click "Download for Windows"
Save the .exe installer to Downloads folder

Step 2: Prepare for Installation

Before running the installer:

Right-click the downloaded .exe file
Select "Properties"
Check "Unblock" checkbox (if present)
Click "OK"

Step 3: Run Installer

Double-click the installer file
If Windows Defender SmartScreen appears:
Click "More info"
Click "Run anyway"
Follow installation wizard:
Accept license agreement
Choose installation directory (default recommended)
Create desktop shortcut (optional)
Click "Install"

Step 4: Launch Meetily

Click "Finish" in installer
Meetily launches automatically
Or: Find in Start Menu → "Meetily"
Troubleshooting Windows Defender:

Windows Defender may flag new releases. This is normal for open-source software without code signing certificates. You can:

Click "More info" → "Run anyway" (safe for official releases)
Add exception in Windows Defender if needed
Verify download hash matches official release (for extra security)
Method 2: GitHub Releases (For More Control)

For developers who want to verify releases, check changelogs, or download specific versions:

Step 1: Download Installer

Visit Meetily GitHub Releases
Download the Windows .exe installer from the latest release
Save to Downloads folder

Step 2: Install Application

Follow the same installation steps as Method 1 above.

Option C: Build from Source (Advanced Users)

For developers who want to customize Meetily:

Prerequisites:
Rust toolchain (rustup)
Node.js 18+
Python 3.10+
Git

Important: Read the build guide first

Before building, read the comprehensive Building Guide for detailed instructions.

Quick Build Steps:
# Clone repository
git clone https://github.com/Zackriya-Solutions/meetily.git
cd meetily/frontend

# Install dependencies
npm install

# Development builds
npm run tauri:dev          # GPU accelerated build (recommended)
npm run tauri dev          # CPU-only build

# Production builds
npm run tauri:build        # or
npm run tauri build

For detailed troubleshooting and platform-specific instructions, see the Building Guide.
Part 2: Initial Configuration

Once Meetily launches, you'll see the setup wizard.

Step 1: Welcome & System Check

The wizard verifies:

✅ Sufficient disk space (10GB+)
✅ Memory requirements (8GB+ RAM)
✅ Audio permissions granted
✅ FFmpeg availability (installed automatically if missing)

Click "Next" when all checks pass.

Step 2: Choose Transcription Engine & Model

Meetily offers two transcription engines-a unique feature no other open-source meeting assistant has:

Option 1: Parakeet Engine (Default, Recommended)

Parakeet is selected by default and provides the best balance of speed and accuracy for most users.

Best For: Real-time transcription with excellent accuracy
Performance: Optimized for fast transcription on modern hardware
Memory: Efficient with Int8 quantization (~1-3GB RAM)
Processing: Significantly faster than Whisper with lower resource usage
Available Parakeet Models:
Lightning (Recommended): Best balance of accuracy and speed for most users
Light: Lighter model for older hardware
Compact: Most compact option with minimal resource usage
Option 2: Whisper Engine (For Maximum Accuracy)

For users who need the highest accuracy and multilingual support.

Best For: Maximum accuracy, broad language support (99+ languages)
Processing: Established, well-tested models
Access: Basic models available by default, advanced Whisper models (Medium, Large) accessible in Advanced Settings
Model	Size	RAM Usage	Speed	Best For
Tiny	~75MB	~1GB	Very Fast	Quick notes, testing
Base	~140MB	~1-2GB	Fast	Casual meetings
Small	~460MB	~2-3GB	Medium	General use
Medium	~1.5GB	~4-5GB	Slower	High accuracy needed (Advanced Settings)
Large v3	~3GB	~8-10GB	Slowest	Professional/critical meetings (Advanced Settings)
Which Should You Choose?

Use Parakeet (Default) if:

You want the best overall experience (recommended for most users)
You need real-time transcription without lag
You want lower CPU usage and battery consumption
You have any modern hardware from the last 5 years

Switch to Whisper if:

You need maximum accuracy for medical, legal, or critical meetings
You require 99+ language support
You have powerful hardware and can afford slower processing
You need specific language optimizations

Default Setup:

Meetily uses Parakeet Lightning by default, which provides excellent accuracy and the fastest transcription speed.

Model Download:
After selecting your preferred engine and model, you'll be prompted to download it
Click "Download Model" to begin the one-time download
The model downloads to your local storage (no internet needed afterward)
You can change models later in Settings if needed
Step 3: Configure Audio Sources
Microphone Selection:
Click "Microphone" dropdown
Select your input device:
Built-in Microphone (laptops)
External USB microphone
Headset microphone
Test: Speak and watch the level meter
System Audio Capture:

This captures audio from your meeting platform (Zoom, Teams, etc.).

macOS:

Meetily uses BlackHole or similar virtual audio device
If not installed: Click "Install Audio Driver"
Follow prompts to install virtual audio device
Restart Meetily after installation

Windows:

Meetily uses Windows WASAPI (built-in)
Click "Enable System Audio"
Grant permissions if prompted
No additional software needed
Test Audio Capture:
Play a YouTube video or music
Check "System Audio" level meter
Verify both microphone and system audio show levels
Step 4: Storage Location

Choose where Meetily saves your transcripts and recordings.

Setting Storage Location:
The storage location is accessible and configurable directly from the Meetily UI
Access it from Settings or from the meeting details page
Click "Change" or "Browse" to select your preferred folder
Choose cloud-synced folder (Dropbox, OneDrive) for automatic backup
Or external drive for extra privacy
What Gets Stored:

Your recordings are saved in the meetily-recordings folder with the following files:

transcript.md - Full meeting transcript
summary.md - AI-generated summary (if enabled)
audio.mp3 - Audio recording (optional)

You can view and change your storage location anytime from Settings or access individual meeting files from the meeting details page.

Step 5: AI Summarization (Optional)

Meetily can generate AI summaries after each meeting. This step is completely optional-transcription works without AI summaries.

Option 1: Local LLM with Ollama (Recommended for Privacy)

Step 1: Install Ollama

Visit ollama.ai and download for your platform
Install Ollama following their instructions
Verify installation: Open terminal and run ollama --version

Step 2: Download a Model

# Recommended lightweight model (800MB)
ollama pull gemma2:2b

# Or for better quality (4.7GB)
ollama pull llama3.2:latest

Step 3: Start Ollama Server

ollama serve

Keep this terminal window open while using Meetily. The server runs at http://localhost:11434

Step 4: Configure in Meetily

Open Meetily → Settings → Model Settings
Select provider: Ollama
Enter model name (e.g., gemma2:2b or llama3.2:latest)
(Optional) Custom endpoint if not using default
Option 2: API-Based LLMs

If you prefer cloud LLMs, Meetily supports:

Anthropic Claude: Settings → Model Settings → Provider: Claude → Enter API key
OpenAI GPT: Settings → Model Settings → Provider: OpenAI → Enter API key
Groq: Settings → Model Settings → Provider: Groq → Enter API key (free tier available)
OpenRouter: Settings → Model Settings → Provider: OpenRouter → Enter API key

Note: Using API-based LLMs means your meeting transcripts are sent to third-party servers for summarization.

Option 3: Skip for Now
You can enable AI summaries later in Settings → Model Settings
Transcription works perfectly without AI summaries
Manual review of transcripts works fine initially

Click "Finish" to complete setup!

Before the Meeting
1. Launch Meetily
Open from Applications (Mac) or Start Menu (Windows)
Ensure it's running (icon in menu bar/system tray)
2. Join Your Meeting
Open Zoom, Teams, Google Meet, Discord, etc.
Join meeting as normal
No special setup needed per platform
3. Start Recording

Community Edition:

In Meetily, click "Record" button to start recording

Pro Edition:

Automatic Meeting Detection: Pro users benefit from automatic meeting detection
Meetily automatically detects when you join a meeting and can start recording
Global Shortcuts: Use Option+X (Mac) / Alt+X (Win) to start/stop recording from anywhere without UI interaction
Floating Recording Panel: Access recording controls via a convenient floating panel
You can also manually start/stop recordings as needed
Configure auto-detection settings in Settings → Meeting Detection
During the Meeting
Real-Time Transcription Display:
Recording in progress...

Good morning everyone, let's start with our product updates.

Sure, I can begin. We've made great progress on the authentication feature...
Controls:
Pause: Temporarily pause recording (excludes segments from transcript)
Bookmark: Mark important moments (adds timestamp marker)
Stop: End recording (triggers AI summary)
Performance Monitoring:
CPU usage indicator
Memory usage
Transcription lag (should be <2 seconds)
After the Meeting
1. Stop Recording
Click "Stop" button in Meetily
Pro users can use global shortcut: Option+X (Mac) / Alt+X (Win)
2. AI Summary Generation (if enabled)
Processing summary...
- Analyzing transcript
- Extracting key points
- Identifying action items
- Detecting decisions

Summary complete! (takes 10-60 seconds)
3. Review & Export

Community Edition:

View transcript in Meetily
Export to Markdown
Copy to clipboard for pasting elsewhere

Audio Upload: Upload existing audio files for transcription

Part 4: Platform-Specific Configuration
Google Meet

No special setup needed!

System audio capture works automatically
Just click "Record" in Meetily before/during meeting

Tips:

Use Chrome or Edge (better audio routing)
Ensure "Use original sound" is enabled in Meet settings
Zoom

Configuration:

Open Zoom → Settings → Audio
Enable "Original Sound for Musicians"
Disable "Suppress background noise" (optional, for better accuracy)

Recording:

Start Meetily recording BEFORE joining Zoom call
Or start after joining (may miss first few seconds)
Microsoft Teams

Configuration:

Open Teams → Settings → Devices
Test your microphone and speakers
Disable "Noise suppression" for better transcription accuracy

Note: Teams' noise suppression can filter out background speakers. Disable for meetings where you need to capture all participants.

Discord

Works perfectly with system audio capture!

No special configuration needed
Captures all voice channels
Real-time transcription during gaming sessions or community calls
Slack Huddles

Configuration:

Join Slack Huddle
Start Meetily recording
System audio captures Huddle audio automatically
Part 5: Advanced Configuration
GPU Acceleration (Automatic)

Meetily automatically detects and uses GPU acceleration if available on your system. No manual configuration needed!

How It Works:
GPU acceleration is detected and enabled automatically at application startup
On macOS: Uses Metal acceleration (built into Apple Silicon M1/M2/M3)
On Windows: Uses compatible NVIDIA CUDA or AMD ROCm if available
Fallback to CPU if no compatible GPU is found
Supported GPUs:
Apple Silicon: M1, M2, M3, M4 (automatic Metal acceleration)
NVIDIA: GTX 1060 or newer (requires CUDA)
AMD: RX 580 or newer (requires ROCm on Linux)

Note: GPU acceleration provides 3-10x faster transcription compared to CPU-only processing. If you experience issues, the app will automatically fall back to CPU processing.

Global Shortcuts (Pro Only)

Pro users have access to global shortcuts that work without UI interaction:

Available Shortcuts:
Start/Stop Recording: Option+X (Mac) / Alt+X (Win)
Works from anywhere on your system, even when Meetily is minimized
No need to switch windows or interact with the UI
Perfect for seamless recording workflow

Configure and customize shortcuts in Settings → Shortcuts (Pro only)

Application Settings
Notification Preferences:
Settings → Preferences → Enable/disable recording notifications
Control which events trigger notifications
Respect Do Not Disturb mode
Storage Locations:
Settings → Preferences → View storage paths
Database location: Where meeting metadata is stored
Models directory: Where transcription models are cached
Recordings folder: Where audio and transcripts are saved
Analytics:
Settings → Preferences → Analytics consent toggle
Opt in/out of usage analytics
Helps improve the app without compromising privacy
Part 6: Troubleshooting Common Issues
Issue 1: "No Audio Detected"

Symptoms: Recording starts but no transcription appears

Solutions:

Check Audio Permissions:
macOS: System Preferences → Security → Microphone → Enable Meetily
Windows: Settings → Privacy → Microphone → Allow Meetily
Test Audio Sources:
Settings → Audio → Test Microphone
Speak and verify level meter moves
Restart Audio Devices:
Unplug and replug headset/microphone
Restart Meetily
Issue 2: "Transcription Too Slow"

Symptoms: Real-time transcription lags 10+ seconds behind speech

Solutions:

Use Smaller Model:
Switch from Large → Medium or Small
Settings → Models → Select smaller model
Enable GPU:
Settings → Performance → Enable GPU
Requires compatible graphics card
Close Other Apps:
Free up RAM and CPU
Close Chrome tabs, video editors, etc.
Issue 3: "Model Download Fails"

Symptoms: Whisper model download stuck or fails

Solutions:

Check Internet Connection:
Ensure stable internet
Large v3 is 3GB download (takes time)
Manual Download:
Download from Hugging Face
Settings → Models → "Import Model" → Select file
Clear Cache:
Settings → Advanced → "Clear Model Cache"
Retry download
Issue 4: "Meetily Won't Launch"

Symptoms: Double-clicking icon does nothing

macOS Solutions:

# Check if app is blocked
xattr -d com.apple.quarantine /Applications/Meetily.app

# Check for errors
/Applications/Meetily.app/Contents/MacOS/Meetily

Windows Solutions:

Run as Administrator: Right-click Meetily → "Run as administrator"
Check Event Viewer: Windows → Event Viewer → Application logs (Look for Meetily errors)
Reinstall: Uninstall via Control Panel, download fresh installer, install again
Issue 5: "Poor Transcription Accuracy"

Symptoms: Many words transcribed incorrectly

Solutions:

Upgrade Whisper Model:
Base → Small or Medium
Accuracy improves significantly
Improve Audio Quality:
Use external microphone (not laptop built-in)
Reduce background noise
Move closer to microphone
Disable Noise Suppression:
In Zoom/Teams settings
Noise suppression can confuse Whisper

Frequently Asked Questions
How much disk space do I need?

For Meetily, you'll need approximately 10GB for initial setup (models: 1-3GB one-time download). Per hour of meeting recording: Audio file (MP3): ~50-100MB, Transcript (Markdown): ~50-200KB. For 10 meetings/week, budget ~5GB/month for audio. Tip: Enable 'Delete audio after transcription' in settings to save space while keeping transcripts.

Can I use Meetily on multiple computers?

Yes! For the free Community Edition, install on multiple devices with transcripts stored locally on each device. Use cloud storage (Dropbox/OneDrive) to sync transcripts between devices. For Enterprise tier, a central server syncs across all team devices, making transcripts accessible from any authenticated device.

Does Meetily work on Linux?

Not yet as a native app. Currently supported platforms: Windows (EXE installer) and macOS (.dmg installer). Linux users can build the app bundle from the open source repo - a native Linux desktop app is in development. Join the waitlist at /granola-for-linux.

What languages does Meetily support?

Whisper supports 99+ languages including English, Spanish, French, German, Italian, Portuguese, Dutch, Polish, Russian, Japanese, Chinese, Korean, Arabic, Hindi, and many more! Accuracy is best for English, with strong performance for other major languages.

Is my meeting data really private?

Yes. Transcription and recordings are always processed locally on your device. Transcripts are saved to your chosen folder. For AI summaries, you choose: fully local models (no internet needed) or bring your own API key. The open-source code can be audited. You have complete control over your data.

Which transcription engine should I choose: Whisper or Parakeet?

Choose Whisper if you need maximum accuracy (medical, legal, critical meetings), require 99+ language support, or have powerful hardware. Choose Parakeet if you have older/slower hardware (8GB RAM or less), need real-time transcription without lag, or want lower CPU usage. For 8GB RAM: Parakeet recommended. For 16GB+ RAM: Whisper Medium or Large for best accuracy.

How do I troubleshoot 'No Audio Detected' errors?

Check audio permissions in System Preferences (macOS) or Settings (Windows). Test audio sources in Meetily Settings → Audio → Test Microphone. Restart audio devices by unplugging and replugging headset/microphone, then restart Meetily. Verify that Meetily has microphone access enabled in your operating system.

Why is transcription too slow on my computer?

Use a smaller Whisper model (switch from Large → Medium or Small in Settings). Enable GPU acceleration if you have a compatible graphics card (Settings → Performance → Enable GPU). Close other applications to free up RAM and CPU. Consider switching to the Parakeet engine which is optimized for speed.

Can I use custom AI models with Meetily?

Yes! Meetily supports multiple transcription models including Whisper variants and custom models via Ollama or llama.cpp. You can fine-tune models for domain-specific terminology (medical, legal, technical) and use custom LLMs for meeting summarization. This flexibility is only possible with open-source, self-hosted architecture.

How does Meetily work with Google Meet, Zoom, and Teams?

Meetily works with all meeting platforms through system audio capture. For Google Meet: No special setup needed. For Zoom: Enable 'Original Sound for Musicians' in Audio settings. For Microsoft Teams: Disable 'Noise suppression' for better accuracy. For Discord: No configuration needed. Just start recording in Meetily before or during your meeting.

Frequently Asked Questions
What makes a meeting transcription tool GDPR compliant?

GDPR-compliant meeting transcription requires: data processing within EU borders or with adequate protection, explicit user consent before recording, data minimization (only collecting necessary information), encryption in transit and at rest, right to access and delete data, data processing agreements with any third-party processors, breach notification procedures, and demonstrable technical measures to protect data. Self-hosted, open-source tools like Meetily offer strong GDPR compliance because transcription runs locally and you control how summaries are processed.

Why are cloud-based meeting transcription tools risky for EU companies?

Cloud-based tools like Otter.ai and Fireflies pose significant risks: data typically stored on US servers subject to CLOUD Act and FISA 702, third-party AI processors may access meeting content, unclear data retention policies, vendor lock-in making compliance audits difficult, potential data breaches affecting thousands of customers simultaneously, and complex DPA requirements. The Schrems II ruling invalidated Privacy Shield, making US-based processors legally problematic for EU data. Self-hosted alternatives eliminate these risks entirely.

What is the best open-source alternative to Otter.ai?

Meetily is the leading open-source alternative to Otter.ai, offering bot-free meeting transcription with local-first processing. Unlike Otter.ai which requires cloud upload and subscription fees, Meetily transcribes locally using Whisper models and offers pluggable AI for summaries (local models or BYOK). It provides similar features (real-time transcription, speaker identification, AI summaries, searchable archives) but with privacy control, zero ongoing costs for Community Edition, and no participant-visible bots.

How do privacy-focused transcription tools compare in accuracy?

Modern privacy-focused tools using Whisper models achieve high accuracy, matching or exceeding cloud-based alternatives. Meetily, Wispr, and other Whisper-based solutions deliver the same transcription quality as Otter.ai and Fireflies since they use the same underlying AI model. The difference is where processing happens: cloud tools send audio to external servers for transcription, while privacy-focused tools transcribe locally. Larger Whisper models (medium, large) provide better accuracy for accents and technical terminology.

Can self-hosted meeting transcription tools integrate with existing enterprise systems?

Yes, usually through APIs rather than ready-made connectors. Meetily Pro includes a local HTTP API, signed webhooks and a built-in command-line tool (all off by default), and exports to Markdown, PDF, DOCX, plain text and JSON. Your own scripts use these to move notes into systems like Notion, Confluence, SharePoint, Salesforce or HubSpot; Meetily has no native connectors for them yet. Enterprise deployments can add custom integrations, and open-source tools allow custom integration development to match your exact requirements without vendor dependencies.

What are the cost differences between privacy-focused and cloud-based meeting tools?

Cloud-based tools have recurring costs: Otter.ai Pro costs $16.99/user/month ($204/year), Fireflies Pro costs $10/user/month billed annually ($120/year), and enterprise plans reach $30-40/user/month. A 100-person team pays $12,000-20,000/year. Privacy-focused self-hosted tools like Meetily are free for the Community Edition (MIT license) with no per-user or per-minute charges for community users. Total cost is just hardware (servers you may already own) and optional implementation support. Return on investment is typically achieved within 3-6 months for teams of 20+ users.

Do privacy-focused meeting transcription tools support multiple languages?

Yes, Whisper-based privacy tools support 99+ languages including English, Spanish, French, German, Italian, Portuguese, Dutch, Polish, Turkish, Russian, Japanese, Chinese, Korean, Arabic, Hindi, and many more. Meetily automatically detects the spoken language and transcribes accordingly. You can also specify the language manually for improved accuracy. Translation features allow transcribing in one language and translating to another, all processed locally without sending data to external translation services.

How do I deploy privacy-focused transcription for a distributed remote team?

For distributed teams, use a hybrid deployment model: each team member installs the desktop client (Meetily) on their local machine for recording and real-time transcription, transcripts automatically sync to a central self-hosted server (on-premise or private cloud), team members access shared transcripts through a web interface, and role-based permissions control access. This approach combines local privacy with centralized management. Cloud hosting on EU servers (Hetzner, OVH) provides GDPR compliance while enabling global access.

What happens to meeting data with self-hosted transcription tools?

With self-hosted tools, you control 100% of the data lifecycle. Audio recordings stay on the user's device unless explicitly uploaded to your private server. Transcripts are stored in your infrastructure (on-premise servers, private cloud, or encrypted cloud storage). You set retention policies determining how long data is kept. Data can be encrypted at rest using your own keys. When deleted, it's truly gone-not retained by a vendor. This level of control is impossible with cloud-based SaaS tools where data flows to vendor servers automatically.

Are privacy-focused meeting tools suitable for regulated industries like healthcare and finance?

Yes, self-hosted privacy-focused tools are ideal for regulated industries. Healthcare organizations use them for HIPAA-compliant telehealth visit documentation, with BAA protection through self-hosting. Financial services use them for FINRA/SEC compliance in client advisory meetings, with proper retention and archival. Legal firms use them for privileged communication with attorney-client privilege protection. Government agencies use them for classified or sensitive discussions. These industries specifically choose self-hosted tools to avoid third-party data processors and maintain audit trails.

Can I migrate my existing transcripts from Otter.ai or Fireflies to a privacy-focused tool?

Yes, migration is possible. Most cloud tools allow transcript export in formats like TXT, DOCX, PDF, or JSON. Meetily can import these files, preserving timestamps, speaker labels, and metadata. The migration process: 1) Export all transcripts from your current tool (may require contacting support for bulk export), 2) Convert to a compatible format (scripts available for batch conversion), 3) Import into Meetily maintaining folder structure and tags, 4) Verify critical transcripts transferred correctly. Plan for a transition period running both systems before fully switching.

What technical expertise is required to deploy self-hosted meeting transcription?

Individual users need minimal technical knowledge-Meetily provides installers for Windows and macOS with setup wizards, and Linux is available via the open source repo for building the app bundle (a native Linux desktop app is in development). For team deployments, IT teams need: basic server administration skills for self-hosted server setup, network configuration knowledge for secure access, and understanding of backup and disaster recovery. Most IT teams can deploy in 2-4 hours. Managed deployment services are available for organizations without dedicated IT resources.

How do privacy-focused transcription tools handle speaker identification?

Privacy-focused tools use several approaches for speaker identification: voice fingerprinting (analyzing voice characteristics without storing biometric data), machine learning models that distinguish speakers by speech patterns, manual speaker labeling during or after meetings, and integration with calendar data to identify meeting participants. Meetily's speaker diarization works offline, processing voice patterns locally without sending biometric data to external services. Accuracy improves over time as the system learns familiar voices, all while keeping data on your infrastructure.

What security features do privacy-focused meeting transcription tools provide?

Enterprise-grade privacy tools offer comprehensive security: end-to-end encryption for data in transit (TLS 1.3), AES-256 encryption at rest with your own keys, optional air-gapped deployment for maximum security, detailed audit logs tracking all access and modifications, role-based access control (RBAC) for granular permissions, two-factor authentication (2FA), automatic security updates through managed deployment, and penetration testing support for compliance requirements. Open-source code allows independent security audits.

Can privacy-focused transcription tools work in hybrid meeting setups (in-person + remote)?

Yes, privacy tools excel at hybrid meetings. For conference room setups, connect Meetily to the room's audio system to capture all speakers (both in-room and remote participants). The AI differentiates between speakers regardless of location. For individual recording, each participant can run their own instance capturing their perspective. Some organizations deploy a dedicated 'transcription laptop' in conference rooms running Meetily to capture all meeting audio. The bot-free nature means no awkward virtual participant appears on the screen in meeting rooms.

The $50K Deal Killed by a Meeting Bot

Sarah, an enterprise sales executive, was minutes into her biggest pitch of the quarter-a $50,000 annual contract with a Fortune 500 company. Then it happened.

"Fireflies Notetaker has joined the meeting"

The client's CFO immediately interrupted: "What's that? Who's recording this call? We didn't consent to external recording."

Despite Sarah's explanation, the damage was done. Trust evaporated. The meeting ended awkwardly. The deal died three days later.

This scenario plays out thousands of times every day across sales teams, legal consultations, healthcare appointments, executive coaching sessions, and HR conversations. Visible meeting bots-those little "AI Assistant" or "Notetaker" names that pop up in your Zoom participant list-create friction, suspicion, and discomfort that traditional meeting AI tools can't avoid.

Why This Matters

Based on feedback from Reddit threads, G2 reviews, and user discussions, visible meeting bots are a common concern across industries:

Sales teams report that visible bots can make prospects uncomfortable and less candid during calls
Legal professionals often avoid cloud-based AI tools due to attorney-client privilege and confidentiality requirements
Healthcare providers cite HIPAA concerns with third-party bots joining patient consultations
Executive coaches note that clients tend to speak less freely when they see a bot recording
What Makes Meeting Bots So Problematic?
1. Professional Image Damage

When "Fireflies Notetaker" or "Otter Assistant" joins your client call, it sends an unintended message: "I can't be bothered to take notes myself, so I'm using a third-party bot to record everything you say."

For enterprise buyers-especially those in regulated industries-this raises immediate red flags about professionalism, data handling, and respect for confidentiality.

2. Trust Issues and Consent Concerns

Even when you verbally disclose that you're recording, seeing a visible third-party bot creates different psychological dynamics than simple recording disclosure. Participants wonder:

Where is this data going?
Who has access to the recording?
Is this compliant with our corporate policies?
What happens if sensitive information is discussed?
Can I trust this external service?
3. Changed Meeting Dynamics

Research in organizational psychology shows that visible surveillance changes behavior. When participants see a bot in the meeting:

People speak more formally and less candidly
Sensitive topics get avoided or discussed "offline"
Creative brainstorming becomes constrained
Authentic relationship-building suffers
Overall meeting effectiveness can suffer as participants hold back
4. Compliance and Legal Risks

For organizations subject to GDPR, HIPAA, attorney-client privilege, or financial services regulations, visible third-party bots create compliance nightmares:

Legal & Compliance Issues with Cloud-Based Meeting Bots:
GDPR Article 44: Data transferred to US-based services (Fireflies, Otter) may violate EU data sovereignty requirements
HIPAA: Business Associate Agreements (BAAs) don't eliminate the risk of PHI exposure via third-party cloud services
Attorney-Client Privilege: Introducing third-party services into legal consultations can potentially waive privilege protections
SOC 2 & ISO 27001: External bots create additional attack surfaces and vendor risk that must be audited
What is Bot-Free Meeting Transcription?

Bot-free meeting transcription uses system audio capture technology instead of joining meetings as a visible participant. Think of it like screen recording for audio-your computer captures what you hear and say, processes it locally, and generates transcripts without any external service joining your call.

How It Works (Technical Overview)
Traditional Cloud-Based Approach (Fireflies, Otter.ai):
Bot joins your Zoom/Teams/Meet call as a visible participant
Bot captures audio stream and sends it to company servers
Audio is transcribed in the cloud using their AI models
Transcript is stored on their servers and accessible via their platform
Your data trains their models (in many cases)
Bot-Free Self-Hosted Approach (Meetily):
Local application captures system audio (like screen recording)
Audio and recordings never leave your device or private network
AI transcription runs locally using open-source models (Whisper)
Transcripts are stored in your encrypted local database
Summaries: choose local AI or bring your own API key
The Self-Hosted Advantage

Self-hosting takes bot-free transcription even further by giving you complete infrastructure control:

Data Sovereignty: Transcription and recordings stay on your device. For summaries, you choose the provider. Critical for GDPR, DSGVO (Germany), RGPD (France), and other privacy laws
Air-Gapped Deployments: Deploy on networks with no internet access-essential for defense contractors, government agencies, and classified environments
Custom Security Policies: Integrate with your existing encryption, key management, and access control systems
No Vendor Lock-In: Open-source code means you can modify, audit, and maintain the system independently
Cost Predictability: Community Edition is free. Pro at $10/user/month is significantly less than cloud alternatives at $10-39/user/month
Bot-Free vs Bot-Required: Feature-by-Feature Comparison
Feature	Meetily (Bot-Free, Self-Hosted)	Fireflies/Otter (Bot-Required, Cloud)
Visibility to Participants	❌ No (completely invisible)	✅ Yes ("Bot Name" visible in participant list)
Professional Image	✅ No disruption or awkwardness	⚠️ Clients see bot, may raise concerns
Data Privacy	✅ Local-first (pluggable AI for summaries)	❌ Sent to third-party servers
Client Comfort Level	✅ Natural conversation flow	⚠️ People speak differently when bot is visible
GDPR Compliance	✅ Full data sovereignty, EU-ready	⚠️ Data processed in US (Privacy Shield concerns)
HIPAA Compliance	✅ No PHI leaves your infrastructure	⚠️ Requires BAA, still third-party risk
Platform Support	✅ Any platform (Zoom, Teams, Meet, etc.)	⚠️ Limited to integrated platforms
Operating Systems	✅ Windows, macOS	⚠️ Platform-dependent
Offline Capability	✅ Works completely offline	❌ Requires internet connection
Open Source	✅ MIT licensed, auditable code	❌ Proprietary, closed-source
Pricing Model	✅ Free (open source) | Pro $10/user/month billed annually	💰 $10-39/user/month subscription
Industries That Need Bot-Free Meeting Transcription
1. Legal Services (Attorney-Client Privilege)

Law firms handle privileged communications daily. Introducing visible third-party bots into client consultations creates potential privilege waiver risks and client discomfort.

Use Case: Corporate M&A negotiations, litigation strategy sessions, client intake interviews
Why Bot-Free Matters: Attorney-client privilege requires confidentiality-third-party services compromise this perception
Compliance Requirements: State bar ethics rules, GDPR (for EU clients), data breach notification laws
2. Healthcare (HIPAA & Patient Privacy)

Telehealth appointments, therapy sessions, and care coordination meetings involve Protected Health Information (PHI). Cloud-based bots create HIPAA compliance challenges and patient trust issues.

Use Case: Virtual consultations, mental health therapy, care team coordination
Why Bot-Free Matters: Patients speak more freely when they don't see external recording services
Compliance Requirements: HIPAA, HITECH Act, state medical privacy laws
3. Financial Services (SOC 2, PCI-DSS)

Wealth advisors, investment banks, and fintech companies discuss sensitive financial data, trading strategies, and client portfolios that cannot risk exposure.

Use Case: Client portfolio reviews, M&A due diligence, trading strategy discussions
Why Bot-Free Matters: Regulatory requirements prohibit sharing client financial data with unapproved vendors
Compliance Requirements: SOC 2 Type II, PCI-DSS, SEC regulations, FINRA rules
4. Enterprise Sales (Deal Confidentiality)

As demonstrated in our opening story, visible bots in sales calls can kill deals. Enterprise buyers expect professionalism and discretion.

Use Case: Enterprise sales calls, contract negotiations, executive briefings
Why Bot-Free Matters: Professional image and client trust directly impact close rates
Benefits: 15-40% improvement in deal progression when clients don't see recording bots (based on sales team surveys)
5. Defense & Government (Classified Information)

Defense contractors, intelligence agencies, and government organizations operate in air-gapped environments where cloud-based tools are prohibited.

Use Case: Classified briefings, defense strategy sessions, intelligence analysis meetings
Why Bot-Free Matters: Cannot use internet-connected services in SCIF (Sensitive Compartmented Information Facility) environments
Compliance Requirements: FedRAMP, ITAR, NIST SP 800-171, DoD Cybersecurity Maturity Model Certification (CMMC)
6. Human Resources (Sensitive Conversations)

Performance reviews, disciplinary actions, and exit interviews require discretion. Visible bots change what employees feel comfortable saying.

Use Case: Performance reviews, conflict mediation, harassment investigations
Why Bot-Free Matters: Psychological safety-employees need to speak candidly without feeling surveilled
Legal Considerations: Employee privacy laws, works council requirements (EU), employment litigation protection
Meetily: Bot-Free, Self-Hosted AI Meeting Transcription

Meetily was built specifically to solve the "bot problem" while delivering enterprise-grade AI transcription and meeting intelligence.

Core Features
Bot-Free Recording: System audio capture-no visible participant joins your meetings
Local AI Processing: Transcription runs on your device using Whisper Large v3 (high accuracy)
Self-Hosted Architecture: Deploy on-premise or in your private cloud (AWS, Azure, GCP)
Cross-Platform Support: Windows and macOS with open source code available for building on other platforms
Open Source: MIT licensed core-audit every line of code yourself
Air-Gapped Capable: Works completely offline after initial setup
Meeting Summaries & Action Items: Local LLM generates insights without cloud processing
Multi-Language Support: 99 languages supported through Whisper models
Encrypted Storage: AES-256 encryption at rest with your own key management
No Vendor Lock-In: Export transcripts in any format, maintain full data ownership
Deployment Options

How to Get Started with Bot-Free Meeting Transcription
Installation (Under 5 Minutes)
macOS:

Download the .dmg installer from the Meetily Downloads page, open it, and drag Meetily to your Applications folder.

Windows:

Visit the Meetily Downloads page, download the Windows installer, run it, and launch the app.

Linux:

Build from source following the documentation.

First Meeting Setup
Launch Meetily and complete the initial configuration wizard
Select AI Models: Choose transcription quality (Base for speed, Large v3 for accuracy)
Configure Audio Sources: Select system audio and microphone inputs
Join Your Meeting: Start your Zoom/Teams/Meet call as normal
Start Recording: Click "Record" in Meetily-no bot joins your meeting
Get AI Transcripts: Real-time transcription appears during the call, summary generates after
System Requirements
Minimum: Intel i5 / AMD Ryzen 5 (Windows) or Apple Silicon (Mac), 8GB RAM, 2GB storage
Recommended: Modern CPU with 16GB+ RAM, GPU acceleration (NVIDIA, AMD, or Apple Silicon), SSD storage
Network: Internet required for initial model download, then works completely offline
The Market is Shifting Toward Bot-Free Solutions

The meeting AI market is experiencing a quiet revolution. While cloud-based tools like Fireflies and Otter continue to dominate consumer and SMB markets, enterprise and regulated industries are increasingly demanding bot-free alternatives.

Market Validation
Growing demand for bot-free tools: Companies like Granola have attracted significant investment by targeting the bot-free meeting niche
Rising search interest for "bot-free meeting transcription" and "no-bot meeting assistant" reflects growing user demand for invisible recording solutions
EU privacy regulations (GDPR, DSGVO, RGPD) are driving enterprises toward self-hosted solutions that eliminate third-party data processing
Zero-trust security architectures are becoming standard in enterprise IT, making cloud-based bots incompatible with security policies
Why Now?

Three technology shifts have made bot-free, self-hosted meeting transcription finally practical:

Whisper v3 achieves cloud-parity accuracy: Open-source speech recognition now matches proprietary models like Google Speech-to-Text and AWS Transcribe
Local LLMs are production-ready: Llama 3.1, Mistral, and Gemma models run efficiently on modern hardware for meeting summarization
Hardware acceleration is mainstream: M-series Macs, modern GPUs, and neural processing units make local inference fast enough for real-time transcription

The result: Bot-free, self-hosted solutions now deliver the same quality as cloud services without the privacy trade-offs.
Take Back Control of Your Meeting Data

If you've ever felt uncomfortable seeing "Fireflies Notetaker" join your client call, or questioned whether your sensitive conversations should be processed by third-party servers, bot-free, self-hosted meeting transcription is the solution.

Meetily brings enterprise-grade AI transcription without the awkwardness, privacy concerns, or compliance headaches of cloud-based bots. Whether you're a solo consultant, a healthcare provider, a law firm, or a Fortune 500 enterprise, you can now have professional meeting intelligence on your terms.

Frequently Asked Questions
What is bot-free meeting transcription?

Bot-free meeting transcription is a method of recording and transcribing meetings without adding a visible bot participant to the call. Instead of a 'Fireflies Notetaker' or 'Otter.ai' joining your meeting, the transcription happens locally on your device by recording system audio. This approach maintains meeting confidentiality and prevents participant discomfort while still providing AI-powered transcription and summaries.

Why are meeting bots problematic for business calls?

Meeting bots cause several critical issues: they can kill deals when prospects see them join, they violate confidentiality in sensitive discussions like M&A or board meetings, they create legal compliance concerns with GDPR and data residency requirements, they make participants uncomfortable reducing conversation quality, and they can be blocked by enterprise security policies. Studies show that visible bots can reduce participant engagement by up to 40%.

How does Meetily record meetings without a bot?

Meetily uses system-level audio capture to record meeting audio directly on your local device. When you join a meeting with Google Meet, Zoom, Teams, or Discord, Meetily captures the system audio and microphone input without joining as a participant. Recording and transcription (using Whisper.cpp) happen entirely on your computer. For AI summaries, you choose: local models or your own API key.

Is bot-free transcription GDPR and HIPAA compliant?

Yes, Meetily's bot-free approach is designed for GDPR and HIPAA compliance. Transcription and recordings are processed locally on your device. For AI summaries, you choose your provider: fully local models or your own API key. When using local models, no data leaves your infrastructure. The open-source code allows security audits, and you control how your data is handled.

Can participants tell I'm recording with bot-free transcription?

No, Meetily doesn't join the meeting as a visible participant, so there's no bot announcement. However, you should still follow proper meeting etiquette and legal requirements by informing participants that the meeting is being recorded. Many jurisdictions require consent for recording, regardless of the method used. Meetily provides customizable disclosure options to help you maintain transparency while avoiding the disruption of visible bots.

What meeting platforms work with bot-free transcription?

Meetily's bot-free transcription works with all major meeting platforms including Google Meet, Zoom, Microsoft Teams, Discord, Slack Huddles, and any other platform that outputs audio through your system. Since it captures system audio rather than joining as a participant, it's platform-agnostic. This includes webinars, conference calls, and even in-person meetings where audio is routed through your computer.

How accurate is bot-free AI transcription compared to Otter.ai or Fireflies?

Meetily uses OpenAI's Whisper model, which provides high accuracy for transcription. Whisper performs well on clear audio in English and supports 99+ languages. The advantage of Meetily's approach is that you can choose which Whisper model to use (tiny, base, small, medium, large) based on your accuracy needs and hardware capabilities. You can even fine-tune models for industry-specific terminology.

Does bot-free transcription work offline?

Yes, Meetily can work completely offline for transcription. Once installed, the Whisper transcription model runs locally on your device without requiring internet connectivity. AI summaries can also run offline if you use local AI models like Llama or Mistral through Ollama. For users who prefer cloud-based summaries, BYOK options are available when connected. Only the initial software download and optional AI model downloads require internet access.

What are the hardware requirements for bot-free local transcription?

Meetily runs on Windows and macOS (Apple Silicon). Linux is available via the open source repo for building the app bundle (a native Linux desktop app is in development - join the waitlist at /granola-for-linux). Minimum requirements: 8GB RAM, and a modern CPU (Intel i5/AMD Ryzen 5 or better) on Windows or an Apple Silicon chip on Mac. For real-time transcription, 16GB RAM recommended. GPU acceleration (NVIDIA/AMD) significantly improves performance but isn't required. The tiny Whisper model works on older hardware, while larger models benefit from newer systems. Most laptops from the last 5 years can handle real-time transcription without issues.

How much does bot-free meeting transcription cost?

Meetily's Community Edition is free and open-source (MIT license) with core features including real-time transcription and AI summaries. Pro is $10/user/month billed annually ($120/year) for speaker identification, auto-detect meetings, and advanced exports. This contrasts with Otter.ai ($16.99/month), Fireflies ($10-19/user/month billed annually), or Zoom AI Companion (requires Business plan at $199/year). Custom pricing is available for teams and enterprises.

Can I use bot-free transcription for sales calls without losing deals?

Absolutely. Many sales professionals switch to Meetily specifically to avoid the deal-killing moment when a bot joins the call. Bot-free transcription lets you capture every detail, generate follow-up emails, and analyze conversation patterns without alarming prospects. You still get AI-powered summaries, action items, and CRM integration capabilities, but without the trust-destroying presence of a visible recording bot that can make prospects immediately defensive.

How do I switch from Fireflies or Otter.ai to bot-free transcription?

Switching to Meetily is straightforward: 1) Download Meetily from GitHub (available for Windows and macOS), 2) Install and run the setup wizard which downloads the Whisper transcription model, 3) Configure your audio sources (system audio + microphone), 4) Join your next meeting normally and click 'Record' in Meetily. Your transcripts appear in real-time, and AI summaries generate automatically after the meeting. Many users run Meetily alongside their existing tools initially before fully switching.

What AI features does bot-free transcription include?

Meetily provides comprehensive AI features: real-time transcription, speaker identification on Pro and Enterprise, automatic AI summaries highlighting key points and decisions, action item extraction with assignee detection, topic segmentation and meeting structure analysis, sentiment analysis for customer calls, searchable transcript archive, and customizable AI prompts for specific use cases. For summaries you can run a local model through Ollama or the bundled on-device model, or connect your own API key for Claude, OpenAI, Groq or OpenRouter. The local path keeps everything on your device.

Is bot-free transcription suitable for enterprise deployment?

Yes, Meetily is designed for enterprise use. It offers self-hosted deployment on your infrastructure ensuring complete data control, user management, role-based access control, audit logging for compliance requirements, bulk deployment via MDM tools, and air-gapped operation for high-security environments. Many enterprises choose Meetily specifically because it avoids third-party data processors required by cloud-based alternatives, simplifying compliance and reducing vendor risk.

Can bot-free transcription integrate with my CRM or project management tools?

Yes, through your own scripts. Meetily Pro can notify a script through signed webhooks when a recording ends or a summary is ready, and its built-in command-line tool and local API export any meeting as Markdown, text or JSON. A short script can then send notes and action items to Salesforce, HubSpot, Jira, Notion or any system with an API. Pro can also connect your AI assistant (Claude, Cursor and other MCP clients) to your meetings. There are no one-click native connectors yet, and the Pro integrations are off by default. The Community Edition exports Markdown, and because it is open source you can build on it.
